    stride_dv_h,
    stride_dv_d,
    stride_gwt,
    stride_gwh,
    stride_sdt,
    stride_sdh,
    scaling,
    T: tl.constexpr,
    WINDOW: tl.constexpr,
    N_DOCS: tl.constexpr,
    CU_SEARCH_ITERS: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_K: tl.constexpr,
    DQK: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_DQK: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_DV: tl.constexpr,
):
    pid_m = tl.program_id(0)
    q_start = pid_m * BLOCK_M
    q_offs = q_start + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
    k_start = q_start - WINDOW + 1
    if k_start < 0:
        k_start = 0
    k_offs = k_start + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_K)
    dqk_offs = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_DQK)
    dv_offs = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_DV)
    q_mask = q_offs < T
    dqk_mask = dqk_offs < DQK
    dv_mask = dv_offs < BLOCK_DV
    doc_start = tl.load(DOC_START_TABLE + q_offs, mask=q_mask, other=0).to(tl.int64)
    doc_end = tl.load(DOC_END_TABLE + q_offs, mask=q_mask, other=0).to(tl.int64)
    rel = q_offs[:, None] - k_offs[None, :]
    valid = (
        q_mask[:, None]
        & (k_offs[None, :] < T)
        & (rel >= 0)
        & (rel < WINDOW)
        & (k_offs[None, :] >= doc_start[:, None])
        & (k_offs[None, :] < doc_end[:, None])
    )

    lse0 = tl.load(LSE + q_offs.to(tl.int64) * stride_lt + 0 * stride_lh, mask=q_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    lse1 = tl.load(LSE + q_offs.to(tl.int64) * stride_lt + 1 * stride_lh, mask=q_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    lse2 = tl.load(LSE + q_offs.to(tl.int64) * stride_lt + 2 * stride_lh, mask=q_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    lse3 = tl.load(LSE + q_offs.to(tl.int64) * stride_lt + 3 * stride_lh, mask=q_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    lse4 = tl.load(LSE + q_offs.to(tl.int64) * stride_lt + 4 * stride_lh, mask=q_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    lse5 = tl.load(LSE + q_offs.to(tl.int64) * stride_lt + 5 * stride_lh, mask=q_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    p0, _w10 = _dc_postonly_probs_loop_head(
        Q, K, POST_W1, q_offs, k_offs, dqk_offs, q_mask, dqk_mask, valid, lse0,
        stride_qt, stride_qh, stride_qd, stride_kt, stride_kh, stride_kd,
        stride_w1t, stride_w1h, scaling, T=T, H=0,
    )
    p1, _w11 = _dc_postonly_probs_loop_head(
        Q, K, POST_W1, q_offs, k_offs, dqk_offs, q_mask, dqk_mask, valid, lse1,
        stride_qt, stride_qh, stride_qd, stride_kt, stride_kh, stride_kd,
        stride_w1t, stride_w1h, scaling, T=T, H=1,
    )
    p2, _w12 = _dc_postonly_probs_loop_head(
        Q, K, POST_W1, q_offs, k_offs, dqk_offs, q_mask, dqk_mask, valid, lse2,
        stride_qt, stride_qh, stride_qd, stride_kt, stride_kh, stride_kd,
        stride_w1t, stride_w1h, scaling, T=T, H=2,
    )
    p3, _w13 = _dc_postonly_probs_loop_head(
        Q, K, POST_W1, q_offs, k_offs, dqk_offs, q_mask, dqk_mask, valid, lse3,
        stride_qt, stride_qh, stride_qd, stride_kt, stride_kh, stride_kd,
        stride_w1t, stride_w1h, scaling, T=T, H=3,
    )
    p4, _w14 = _dc_postonly_probs_loop_head(
        Q, K, POST_W1, q_offs, k_offs, dqk_offs, q_mask, dqk_mask, valid, lse4,
        stride_qt, stride_qh, stride_qd, stride_kt, stride_kh, stride_kd,
        stride_w1t, stride_w1h, scaling, T=T, H=4,
    )
    p5, _w15 = _dc_postonly_probs_loop_head(
        Q, K, POST_W1, q_offs, k_offs, dqk_offs, q_mask, dqk_mask, valid, lse5,
        stride_qt, stride_qh, stride_qd, stride_kt, stride_kh, stride_kd,
        stride_w1t, stride_w1h, scaling, T=T, H=5,
    )

    a_hidden = (
        _w10[:, None] * p0
        + _w11[:, None] * p1
        + _w12[:, None] * p2
        + _w13[:, None] * p3
        + _w14[:, None] * p4
        + _w15[:, None] * p5
    )
    da_acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_K), dtype=tl.float32)
    da_acc = _dc_postonly_corr_bwd_pre_wsmall_dm_head(
        V, DO, POST_W2, DV, GPOST_W2, da_acc, a_hidden, q_offs, k_offs, rel, valid, q_mask, dv_offs, dv_mask,
        stride_vt, stride_vh, stride_vd, stride_dot, stride_doh, stride_dod,
        stride_w2t, stride_w2h, stride_dv_t, stride_dv_h, stride_dv_d,
        stride_gwt, stride_gwh, T=T, H=0,
    )
    da_acc = _dc_postonly_corr_bwd_pre_wsmall_dm_head(
        V, DO, POST_W2, DV, GPOST_W2, da_acc, a_hidden, q_offs, k_offs, rel, valid, q_mask, dv_offs, dv_mask,
        stride_vt, stride_vh, stride_vd, stride_dot, stride_doh, stride_dod,
        stride_w2t, stride_w2h, stride_dv_t, stride_dv_h, stride_dv_d,
        stride_gwt, stride_gwh, T=T, H=1,
    )
    da_acc = _dc_postonly_corr_bwd_pre_wsmall_dm_head(
        V, DO, POST_W2, DV, GPOST_W2, da_acc, a_hidden, q_offs, k_offs, rel, valid, q_mask, dv_offs, dv_mask,
        stride_vt, stride_vh, stride_vd, stride_dot, stride_doh, stride_dod,
        stride_w2t, stride_w2h, stride_dv_t, stride_dv_h, stride_dv_d,
        stride_gwt, stride_gwh, T=T, H=2,
    )
    da_acc = _dc_postonly_corr_bwd_pre_wsmall_dm_head(
        V, DO, POST_W2, DV, GPOST_W2, da_acc, a_hidden, q_offs, k_offs, rel, valid, q_mask, dv_offs, dv_mask,
        stride_vt, stride_vh, stride_vd, stride_dot, stride_doh, stride_dod,
        stride_w2t, stride_w2h, stride_dv_t, stride_dv_h, stride_dv_d,
        stride_gwt, stride_gwh, T=T, H=3,
    )
    da_acc = _dc_postonly_corr_bwd_pre_wsmall_dm_head(
        V, DO, POST_W2, DV, GPOST_W2, da_acc, a_hidden, q_offs, k_offs, rel, valid, q_mask, dv_offs, dv_mask,
        stride_vt, stride_vh, stride_vd, stride_dot, stride_doh, stride_dod,
        stride_w2t, stride_w2h, stride_dv_t, stride_dv_h, stride_dv_d,
        stride_gwt, stride_gwh, T=T, H=4,
    )
    da_acc = _dc_postonly_corr_bwd_pre_wsmall_dm_head(
        V, DO, POST_W2, DV, GPOST_W2, da_acc, a_hidden, q_offs, k_offs, rel, valid, q_mask, dv_offs, dv_mask,
        stride_vt, stride_vh, stride_vd, stride_dot, stride_doh, stride_dod,
        stride_w2t, stride_w2h, stride_dv_t, stride_dv_h, stride_dv_d,
        stride_gwt, stride_gwh, T=T, H=5,
    )
    tl.store(
        DA_BUF + q_offs[:, None].to(tl.int64) * stride_at + rel.to(tl.int64) * stride_aw,
        da_acc,
        mask=valid,
    )

    _dc_postonly_corr_bwd_pre_wsmall_soft_head(
        POST_W1, SOFT_DOT, GPOST_W1, p0, da_acc, q_offs, q_mask,
        stride_w1t, stride_w1h, stride_sdt, stride_sdh, stride_gwt, stride_gwh, H=0,
    )
    _dc_postonly_corr_bwd_pre_wsmall_soft_head(
        POST_W1, SOFT_DOT, GPOST_W1, p1, da_acc, q_offs, q_mask,
        stride_w1t, stride_w1h, stride_sdt, stride_sdh, stride_gwt, stride_gwh, H=1,
    )
    _dc_postonly_corr_bwd_pre_wsmall_soft_head(
        POST_W1, SOFT_DOT, GPOST_W1, p2, da_acc, q_offs, q_mask,
        stride_w1t, stride_w1h, stride_sdt, stride_sdh, stride_gwt, stride_gwh, H=2,
    )
    _dc_postonly_corr_bwd_pre_wsmall_soft_head(
        POST_W1, SOFT_DOT, GPOST_W1, p3, da_acc, q_offs, q_mask,
        stride_w1t, stride_w1h, stride_sdt, stride_sdh, stride_gwt, stride_gwh, H=3,
    )
    _dc_postonly_corr_bwd_pre_wsmall_soft_head(
        POST_W1, SOFT_DOT, GPOST_W1, p4, da_acc, q_offs, q_mask,
        stride_w1t, stride_w1h, stride_sdt, stride_sdh, stride_gwt, stride_gwh, H=4,
    )
    _dc_postonly_corr_bwd_pre_wsmall_soft_head(
        POST_W1, SOFT_DOT, GPOST_W1, p5, da_acc, q_offs, q_mask,
        stride_w1t, stride_w1h, stride_sdt, stride_sdh, stride_gwt, stride_gwh, H=5,
    )

@triton.jit
def _dc_postonly_corr_bwd_qk_wsmall_kernel(
    Q,
    K,
    POST_W1,
    DOC_START_TABLE,
    DOC_END_TABLE,
    DA_BUF,
    SOFT_DOT,
    LSE,
    DQ,
    DK,
    stride_qt,
    stride_qh,
    stride_qd,
    stride_kt,
    stride_kh,
    stride_kd,
    stride_wt,
    stride_wh,
    stride_at,
    stride_aw,
    stride_sdt,
    stride_sdh,
    stride_lt,
    stride_lh,
    stride_dq_t,
    stride_dq_h,
    stride_dq_d,
    stride_dk_t,
    stride_dk_h,
    stride_dk_d,
    scaling,
    T: tl.constexpr,
    WINDOW: tl.constexpr,
    N_DOCS: tl.constexpr,
    CU_SEARCH_ITERS: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_K: tl.constexpr,
    DQK: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_DQK: tl.constexpr,
):
    pid_m = tl.program_id(0)
    h = tl.program_id(1)
    q_start = pid_m * BLOCK_M
    q_offs = q_start + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
    k_start = q_start - WINDOW + 1
    if k_start < 0:
        k_start = 0
    k_offs = k_start + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_K)
    dqk_offs = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_DQK)
    q_mask = q_offs < T
    dqk_mask = dqk_offs < DQK
    doc_start = tl.load(DOC_START_TABLE + q_offs, mask=q_mask, other=0).to(tl.int64)
    doc_end = tl.load(DOC_END_TABLE + q_offs, mask=q_mask, other=0).to(tl.int64)
    rel = q_offs[:, None] - k_offs[None, :]
    valid = (
        q_mask[:, None]
        & (k_offs[None, :] < T)
        & (rel >= 0)
        & (rel < WINDOW)
        & (k_offs[None, :] >= doc_start[:, None])
        & (k_offs[None, :] < doc_end[:, None])
    )
    q = tl.load(
        Q + q_offs[:, None].to(tl.int64) * stride_qt + h * stride_qh
        + dqk_offs[None, :].to(tl.int64) * stride_qd,
        mask=q_mask[:, None] & dqk_mask[None, :],
        other=0.0,
    )
    k_load_offs = tl.minimum(k_offs, T - 1)
    k = tl.load(
        K + k_load_offs[:, None].to(tl.int64) * stride_kt + h * stride_kh
        + dqk_offs[None, :].to(tl.int64) * stride_kd,
        mask=dqk_mask[None, :],
        other=0.0,
    )
    lse = tl.load(
        LSE + q_offs.to(tl.int64) * stride_lt + h * stride_lh,
        mask=q_mask,
        other=0.0,
    ).to(tl.float32)
    score = tl.dot(q, tl.trans(k), input_precision="tf32") * (scaling * 1.4426950408889634)
    probs = tl.exp2(score - (lse * 1.4426950408889634)[:, None])
    probs = tl.where(valid, probs, 0.0)
    post_w1 = tl.load(
        POST_W1 + q_offs.to(tl.int64) * stride_wt + h * stride_wh,
        mask=q_mask,
        other=0.0,
    ).to(tl.float32)
    da = tl.load(
        DA_BUF + q_offs[:, None].to(tl.int64) * stride_at + rel.to(tl.int64) * stride_aw,
        mask=valid,
        other=0.0,
    ).to(tl.float32)
    soft_dot = tl.load(
        SOFT_DOT + q_offs.to(tl.int64) * stride_sdt + h * stride_sdh,
        mask=q_mask,
        other=0.0,
    ).to(tl.float32)
    dp = post_w1[:, None] * da
    ds = probs * (dp - soft_dot[:, None])
    dq_blk = tl.dot(ds.to(k.dtype), k, input_precision="tf32") * scaling
    dk_blk = tl.dot(tl.trans(ds.to(q.dtype)), q, input_precision="tf32") * scaling
    tl.store(
        DQ + q_offs[:, None].to(tl.int64) * stride_dq_t + h * stride_dq_h
        + dqk_offs[None, :].to(tl.int64) * stride_dq_d,
        dq_blk,
        mask=q_mask[:, None] & dqk_mask[None, :],
    )
    tl.atomic_add(
        DK + k_offs[:, None].to(tl.int64) * stride_dk_t + h * stride_dk_h
        + dqk_offs[None, :].to(tl.int64) * stride_dk_d,
        dk_blk,
        sem="relaxed",
        mask=(k_offs[:, None] < T) & dqk_mask[None, :],
    )

def _is_supported_postonly_triton(
    q: torch.Tensor,
    k: torch.Tensor,
    v: torch.Tensor,
    post_w1: torch.Tensor,
    post_w2: torch.Tensor,
    seq_lens: torch.Tensor,
) -> bool:
    return (
        q.is_cuda
        and k.is_cuda
        and v.is_cuda
        and post_w1.is_cuda
        and post_w2.is_cuda
        and seq_lens.is_cuda
        and q.ndim == 4
        and k.shape == q.shape
        and v.shape[:3] == q.shape[:3]
        and post_w1.shape == q.shape[:3]
        and post_w2.shape == q.shape[:3]
        and q.shape[0] == 1
        and seq_lens.numel() >= 2
        and q.dtype in (torch.float16, torch.bfloat16)
        and k.dtype == q.dtype
        and v.dtype == q.dtype
        and post_w1.dtype == q.dtype
        and post_w2.dtype == q.dtype
    )

def _normalize_block_m(block_m: int) -> int:
    block_m = max(8, min(64, int(block_m)))
    if block_m & (block_m - 1):
        block_m = 1 << (block_m - 1).bit_length()
    return block_m


def _dc_attention_postonly_correction_add_base_forward_impl(
    q: torch.Tensor,
    k: torch.Tensor,
    v: torch.Tensor,
    post_w1: torch.Tensor,
    post_w2: torch.Tensor,
    cu_seqlens: torch.Tensor,
    scaling: float,
    window: int,
    block_m: int,
    return_aux: bool = False,
    base_out: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> torch.Tensor | tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
    # Q/K may be an unpadded view of the 128-wide FA3 tensors.  Every kernel
    # below uses the explicit strides, so materializing those views only adds
    # two full read/write copies.
    v = v.contiguous()
    cu_seqlens = cu_seqlens.contiguous()

    B, T, H, DQK = q.shape
    D = v.shape[-1]
    assert B == 1 and H == 6 and D == 128
    assert int(window) > 0 and int(window) <= 128
    assert base_out is not None

    n_docs = int(cu_seqlens.numel() - 1)
    cu_search_iters = max(1, n_docs.bit_length())
    doc_start_table, doc_end_table = _build_doc_bounds_tables(
        cu_seqlens, T, n_docs, cu_search_iters
    )
    block_m = _normalize_block_m(block_m)
    block_dqk = triton.next_power_of_2(DQK)
    block_dv = triton.next_power_of_2(D)

    if base_out.ndim == 3:
        assert base_out.shape == (T, H, D)
        base_view = base_out
        base_head_stride, base_t_stride, base_d_stride = base_view.stride(1), base_view.stride(0), base_view.stride(2)
        out = torch.empty_like(base_view)
        out_head_stride, out_t_stride, out_d_stride = out.stride(1), out.stride(0), out.stride(2)
    else:
        assert base_out.shape == (B, T, H, D)
        base_view = base_out
        base_head_stride, base_t_stride, base_d_stride = base_view.stride(2), base_view.stride(1), base_view.stride(3)
        out = torch.empty_like(base_view)
        out_head_stride, out_t_stride, out_d_stride = out.stride(2), out.stride(1), out.stride(3)

    block_k = int(DC_POSTONLY_CORR_FWD_WSMALL_BLOCK_K)
    min_block_k = 1 << (int(window) + block_m - 2).bit_length()
    block_k = max(128, min(256, block_k))
    if block_k & (block_k - 1):
        block_k = 1 << (block_k - 1).bit_length()
    block_k = max(block_k, min_block_k)

    lse_for_bwd = torch.empty((B, T, H), device=q.device, dtype=torch.float32) if return_aux else out
    stride_lt, stride_lh = (lse_for_bwd.stride(1), lse_for_bwd.stride(2)) if return_aux else (0, 0)
    a_buf_placeholder = out
    grid_m = (triton.cdiv(T, block_m),)
    _dc_postonly_corr_fwd_wsmall_cached_kernel[grid_m](
        q,
        k,
        v,
        post_w1,
        post_w2,
        doc_start_table,
        doc_end_table,
        a_buf_placeholder,
        lse_for_bwd,
        base_view,
        out,
        q.stride(1),
        q.stride(2),
        q.stride(3),
        k.stride(1),
        k.stride(2),
        k.stride(3),
        v.stride(1),
        v.stride(2),
        v.stride(3),
        post_w1.stride(1),
        post_w1.stride(2),
        post_w2.stride(1),
        post_w2.stride(2),
        0,
        0,
        stride_lt,
        stride_lh,
        base_head_stride,
        base_t_stride,
        base_d_stride,
        out_head_stride,
        out_t_stride,
        out_d_stride,
        float(scaling),
        T=T,
        WINDOW=int(window),
        N_DOCS=n_docs,
        CU_SEARCH_ITERS=cu_search_iters,
        BLOCK_M=block_m,
        BLOCK_K=block_k,
        DQK=DQK,
        BLOCK_DQK=block_dqk,
        BLOCK_DV=block_dv,
        STORE_AUX=return_aux,
        STORE_A_BUF=False,
        ADD_BASE=True,
        num_warps=(8 if block_m >= 32 else 4),
        num_stages=3,
    )
    if return_aux:
        return out, lse_for_bwd, doc_start_table, doc_end_table
    return out


def _dc_attention_postonly_correction_add_base_backward_impl(
    q: torch.Tensor,
    k: torch.Tensor,
    v: torch.Tensor,
    post_w1: torch.Tensor,
    post_w2: torch.Tensor,
    cu_seqlens: torch.Tensor,
    doc_start_table: torch.Tensor,
    doc_end_table: torch.Tensor,
    softmax_lse: torch.Tensor,
    grad_out: torch.Tensor,
    scaling: float,
    window: int,
    skip_qk_grad_requested: bool = False,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
    # Preserve strided, reduced-width Q/K views; the kernels consume their
    # explicit strides and do not require dense token/head packing.
    v = v.contiguous()
    cu_seqlens = cu_seqlens.contiguous()
    doc_start_table = doc_start_table.contiguous()
    doc_end_table = doc_end_table.contiguous()
    softmax_lse = softmax_lse.contiguous()

    B, T, H, DQK = q.shape
    D = v.shape[-1]
    assert B == 1 and H == 6 and D == 128
    assert int(window) > 0 and int(window) <= 128
    if grad_out.ndim == 3:
        assert grad_out.shape == (T, H, D)
        stride_dot, stride_doh, stride_dod = grad_out.stride(0), grad_out.stride(1), grad_out.stride(2)
    else:
        assert grad_out.shape == (B, T, H, D)
        stride_dot, stride_doh, stride_dod = grad_out.stride(1), grad_out.stride(2), grad_out.stride(3)
    if softmax_lse.ndim == 2:
        stride_lt, stride_lh = softmax_lse.stride(1), softmax_lse.stride(0)
    else:
        stride_lt, stride_lh = softmax_lse.stride(-2), softmax_lse.stride(-1)

    n_docs = int(cu_seqlens.numel() - 1)
    cu_search_iters = max(1, n_docs.bit_length())
    small_block_m = 16
    small_block_k = int(DC_POSTONLY_CORR_BWD_WSMALL_BLOCK_K)
    dv = torch.zeros_like(v)
    da_buf = torch.empty((B, T, int(window)), device=q.device, dtype=torch.float32)
    soft_dot = torch.empty((B, T, H), device=q.device, dtype=torch.float32)
    gpost_w1 = torch.empty_like(post_w1, memory_format=torch.contiguous_format)
    gpost_w2 = torch.empty_like(post_w2, memory_format=torch.contiguous_format)

    grid_m = (triton.cdiv(T, small_block_m),)
    _dc_postonly_corr_bwd_pre_wsmall_kernel[grid_m](
        q,
        k,
        v,
        grad_out,
        post_w1,
        post_w2,
        doc_start_table,
        doc_end_table,
        softmax_lse,
        da_buf,
        soft_dot,
        dv,
        gpost_w1,
        gpost_w2,
        q.stride(1),
        q.stride(2),
        q.stride(3),
        k.stride(1),
        k.stride(2),
        k.stride(3),
        v.stride(1),
        v.stride(2),
        v.stride(3),
        stride_dot,
        stride_doh,
        stride_dod,
        post_w1.stride(1),
        post_w1.stride(2),
        post_w2.stride(1),
        post_w2.stride(2),
        da_buf.stride(1),
        da_buf.stride(2),
        stride_lt,
        stride_lh,
        dv.stride(1),
        dv.stride(2),
        dv.stride(3),
        gpost_w1.stride(1),
        gpost_w1.stride(2),
        soft_dot.stride(1),
        soft_dot.stride(2),
        float(scaling),
        T=T,
        WINDOW=int(window),
        N_DOCS=n_docs,
        CU_SEARCH_ITERS=cu_search_iters,
        BLOCK_M=small_block_m,
        BLOCK_K=small_block_k,
        DQK=DQK,
        BLOCK_DQK=triton.next_power_of_2(DQK),
        BLOCK_DV=128,
        num_warps=int(DC_POSTONLY_CORR_BWD_WSMALL_PRE_WARPS),
        num_stages=int(DC_POSTONLY_CORR_BWD_WSMALL_PRE_STAGES),
    )

    if skip_qk_grad_requested:
        return None, None, dv, gpost_w1, gpost_w2

    dq = torch.empty_like(q)
    dk = torch.zeros_like(k)
    grid_qk_small = (triton.cdiv(T, small_block_m), H)
    _dc_postonly_corr_bwd_qk_wsmall_kernel[grid_qk_small](
        q,
        k,
        post_w1,
        doc_start_table,
        doc_end_table,
        da_buf,
        soft_dot,
        softmax_lse,
        dq,
        dk,
        q.stride(1),
        q.stride(2),
        q.stride(3),
        k.stride(1),
        k.stride(2),
        k.stride(3),
        post_w1.stride(1),
        post_w1.stride(2),
        da_buf.stride(1),
        da_buf.stride(2),
        soft_dot.stride(1),
        soft_dot.stride(2),
        stride_lt,
        stride_lh,
        dq.stride(1),
        dq.stride(2),
        dq.stride(3),
        dk.stride(1),
        dk.stride(2),
        dk.stride(3),
        float(scaling),
        T=T,
        WINDOW=int(window),
        N_DOCS=n_docs,
        CU_SEARCH_ITERS=cu_search_iters,
        BLOCK_M=small_block_m,
        BLOCK_K=small_block_k,
        DQK=DQK,
        BLOCK_DQK=triton.next_power_of_2(DQK),
        num_warps=int(DC_POSTONLY_CORR_BWD_WSMALL_QK_WARPS),
        num_stages=int(DC_POSTONLY_CORR_BWD_WSMALL_QK_STAGES),
    )
    return dq, dk, dv, gpost_w1, gpost_w2


class _DCPostOnlyCorrectionAddBaseTriton(torch.autograd.Function):
    @staticmethod
    def forward(
        ctx,
        base_out: torch.Tensor,
        q: torch.Tensor,
        k: torch.Tensor,
        v: torch.Tensor,
        post_w1: torch.Tensor,
        post_w2: torch.Tensor,
        cu_seqlens: torch.Tensor,
        softmax_lse: torch.Tensor,
        scaling: float,
        window: int,
        block_m: int,
        block_n: int,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        needs_bwd = any(ctx.needs_input_grad[1:6])
        ctx.scaling = float(scaling)
        ctx.window = int(window)
        ctx.base_ndim = int(base_out.ndim)
        ctx.needs_bwd = needs_bwd
        out_aux = _dc_attention_postonly_correction_add_base_forward_impl(
            q,
            k,
            v,
            post_w1,
            post_w2,
            cu_seqlens,
            scaling,
            window,
            block_m,
            return_aux=needs_bwd,
            base_out=base_out,
        )
        if needs_bwd:
            out, lse_for_bwd, doc_start_table, doc_end_table = out_aux
            ctx.save_for_backward(
                q, k, v, post_w1, post_w2, cu_seqlens,
                doc_start_table, doc_end_table, lse_for_bwd,
            )
            return out
        ctx.save_for_backward()
        return out_aux

    @staticmethod
    def backward(ctx, grad_out: torch.Tensor):
        grad_base = grad_out if ctx.needs_input_grad[0] else None
        if not ctx.needs_bwd:
            return (grad_base, None, None, None, None, None, None, None, None, None, None, None)
        q, k, v, post_w1, post_w2, cu_seqlens, doc_start_table, doc_end_table, softmax_lse = ctx.saved_tensors
        grad_corr = grad_out
        grads = _dc_attention_postonly_correction_add_base_backward_impl(
            q,
            k,
            v,
            post_w1,
            post_w2,
            cu_seqlens,
            doc_start_table,
            doc_end_table,
            softmax_lse,
            grad_corr,
            ctx.scaling,
            ctx.window,
            not (ctx.needs_input_grad[1] or ctx.needs_input_grad[2]),
        )
        return (grad_base, *grads, None, None, None, None, None, None)


def dc_attention_postonly_nodd_correction_add_base_triton(
    base_out: torch.Tensor,
    q: torch.Tensor,
    k: torch.Tensor,
    v: torch.Tensor,
    dc_weights: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor],
    softmax_lse: torch.Tensor | None,
    scaling: float,
    window: int = None,
    seq_lens: torch.Tensor = None,
    block_m: int = 16,
    block_n: int = 32,
) -> torch.Tensor:
    post_w1, post_w2 = dc_weights
    # Q/K may be narrower than V (reduced-width QK projections); the correction
    # output and base_out carry the V width.
    B, T, H, DQK = q.shape
    D = v.shape[-1]
    if window is None:
        window = 112
    if seq_lens is None:
        seq_lens = torch.tensor([0, T], device=q.device, dtype=torch.int32) if B == 1 else None
    elif seq_lens.numel() == B and B == 1:
        seq_lens = torch.tensor([0, int(seq_lens[0].item())], device=q.device, dtype=torch.int32)

    base_shape_ok = base_out.shape == (T, H, D) or base_out.shape == (B, T, H, D)
    if (
        seq_lens is None
        or not base_shape_ok
        or not _is_supported_postonly_triton(q, k, v, post_w1, post_w2, seq_lens)
        or H != 6
        or D != 128
        or DQK <= 0
        or DQK > D
        or int(window) <= 0
        or int(window) > 128
        or (softmax_lse is not None and softmax_lse.numel() != 0)
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("unsupported fused base+post-only no-DD DC correction Triton shape")
    if softmax_lse is None:
        softmax_lse = torch.empty(0, device=q.device, dtype=torch.float32)

    return _DCPostOnlyCorrectionAddBaseTriton.apply(
        base_out,
        q,
        k,
        v,
        post_w1,
        post_w2,
        seq_lens,
        softmax_lse,
        float(scaling),
        int(window),
        int(block_m),
        int(block_n),
    )


----------------------------------------
# scratch nanogpt.csa2_flex
----------------------------------------

"""CSA2's KV layout (DeepSeek-V4.1 CSA2) in record 91's attention, with csa2_select's
parameter-free picks. Each query attends in one softmax to:
- its own layer's keys within WINDOW tokens of its document (the local window);
- top_k entries of a global KV shared by a group of layers: one K=V head, entry e a
  gated pool of tokens [e m, (e + 1) m) (the archived Csa2Compressor, no norm
  weight), picked by csa2_select from the layer's own q.
Entry keys are the entries rotated by record 91's RoPE (its yarn tables; pairs
(2i, 2i + 1), y = f1 x + f2 x_flip) to the entry's first token. Values are the
unrotated entries, so a cache keeps one D-channel vector per entry.

attention() is flex attention (tournament_fast.attend) over the row's keys followed
by the entries. Each 128-query tile reads its own row tile, the tile before it, and
the tiles of entries its documents have finished.
Shapes: q, k, v (T, H, D) for one packed row, T a multiple of 128; entries (N, D).
"""

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch import Tensor

from nanogpt.tournament_fast import TILE, MaskMod, attend

WINDOW = 128  # CSA2's sliding window


def starts(cu_seqlens: Tensor, length: int) -> Tensor:
    """(length,) int64 row position of each token's document start."""
    cu = cu_seqlens.long()
    at = torch.searchsorted(cu, torch.arange(length, device=cu.device), right=True)
    return cu[at - 1]


def compress(x: Tensor, w_kv: Tensor, w_gate: Tensor, m: int) -> Tensor:
    """(T // m, D) entries of x (T, C), w (D, C): rms_norm of the softmax(x w_gate)
    weighted sum of x w_kv over each window of m tokens."""
    n = x.shape[0] // m
    x = x[: n * m]
    kv = F.linear(x, w_kv).view(n, m, -1)
    gate = F.linear(x, w_gate).view(n, m, -1).softmax(1, dtype=torch.float32)
    return F.rms_norm((kv * gate.type_as(kv)).sum(1), (kv.shape[-1],))


def rotate(entries: Tensor, m: int, factor1: Tensor, factor2: Tensor) -> Tensor:
    """entries (N, D) rotated to token e m by record 91's RoPE tables (rows =
    positions) on the first factor1.shape[1] channels; the rest unchanged."""
    n, width = entries.shape[0], factor1.shape[1]
    x = entries[:, :width].float()
    flip = x.view(n, -1, 2).flip(-1).view(n, width)
    f1, f2 = factor1[: n * m : m].float(), factor2[: n * m : m].float()
    return torch.cat([(f1 * x + f2 * flip).type_as(entries), entries[:, width:]], 1)


def words(entry: Tensor, valid: Tensor, entries: int) -> Tensor:
    """(T, entries / 32) int32: bit e % 32 of word e // 32 set per valid pick e."""
    out = entry.new_zeros(entry.shape[0], -(-entries // 32), dtype=torch.int32)
    one = torch.ones_like(entry, dtype=torch.int32)
    bit = (one << (entry % 32).int()) * valid  # distinct bits: the sum is their or
    return out.scatter_add_(1, entry // 32, bit)


@torch.library.custom_op("csa2_flex::mask_inputs", mutates_args=())
def mask_inputs(
    entry: Tensor, valid: Tensor, cu_seqlens: Tensor, length: int, entries: int
) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:
    """starts() and words() as one opaque op: under torch.compile, flex's mask_mod
    cannot index buffers computed in the graph (inductor: FlexibleLayout)."""
    return starts(cu_seqlens, length), words(entry, valid, entries)


@mask_inputs.register_fake
def _(
    entry: Tensor, valid: Tensor, cu_seqlens: Tensor, length: int, entries: int
) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:
    return (
        entry.new_empty(length),
        entry.new_empty(entry.shape[0], -(-entries // 32), dtype=torch.int32),
    )


def tiles(begin: Tensor, length: int, n: int, m: int) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:
    """flex's (1, 1, tiles) KV tile counts and (1, 1, tiles, KV tiles) indices
    (from_kv_blocks reads the last size as the KV tile count): query tile i reads
    row tiles i - 1 and i, then the tiles holding entries from its first query's
    document start to its last query's finished entries."""
    rows = length // TILE
    i = torch.arange(rows, device=begin.device)
    near = 1 + (i > 0).long()
    lo = -(-begin[i * TILE] // m)
    hi = torch.clamp((i + 1) * TILE // m, max=n)
    first = rows + lo // TILE
    far = torch.where(hi > lo, rows + (hi - 1) // TILE - first + 1, 0)
    slot = torch.arange(rows + -(-n // TILE), device=begin.device)
    index = torch.where(
        slot < near[:, None],
        (i - near + 1)[:, None] + slot,
        first[:, None] + slot - near[:, None],
    )
    count = near + far
    index = torch.where(slot < count[:, None], index, 0)
    return count[None, None].int(), index[None, None].int()


def mask(begin: Tensor, bits: Tensor, length: int) -> MaskMod:
    """Flex mask_mod: a row key in the query's window of its document, or a picked
    entry (key length + e)."""

    def keep(b: Tensor, h: Tensor, q_idx: Tensor, kv_idx: Tensor) -> Tensor:
        near = (kv_idx <= q_idx) & (q_idx - kv_idx < WINDOW) & (kv_idx >= begin[q_idx])
        e = (kv_idx - length).clamp(min=0)
        bit = (bits[q_idx, e // 32] >> (e % 32)) & 1
        return near | ((kv_idx >= length) & (bit != 0))

    return keep


def attention(
    q: Tensor,
    k: Tensor,
    v: Tensor,
    keys: Tensor,
    values: Tensor,
    entry: Tensor,
    valid: Tensor,
    cu_seqlens: Tensor,
    scale: float,
    m: int = 4,
) -> Tensor:
    """(T, H, D) softmax over each query's window keys and picked entries.

    Args:
        q, k, v: (T, H, D) one packed row, T a multiple of 128.
        keys, values: (N, D) rotated and unrotated entries (one head, all heads).
        entry, valid: (T, top_k) csa2_select picks.
        cu_seqlens: document starts, may repeat T at its end.
        scale: logit scale.
        m: tokens per entry.
    """
    length, heads, _ = q.shape
    n = keys.shape[0]
    pad = -n % TILE
    keys, values = (F.pad(x, (0, 0, 0, pad)) for x in (keys, values))
    begin, bits = mask_inputs(entry, valid, cu_seqlens, length, n + pad)
    count, index = tiles(begin, length, n, m)
    k = torch.cat([k, keys[:, None].expand(-1, heads, -1)])
    v = torch.cat([v, values[:, None].expand(-1, heads, -1)])
    return attend(q, k, v, scale, count, index, mask(begin, bits, length))


----------------------------------------
# scratch nanogpt.csa2_select
----------------------------------------

"""Parameter-free picks for CSA2's compressed KV (DeepSeek-V4.1 CSA2): each query's
top_k entries of the shared K=V head, scored by the logit summed over the query
heads, (sum_h q_h) . kv. Replaces CSA2's learned indexer: no indexer weights, no
indexer loss, no indexer K in the cache.

exact: the top_k entries by that score.
tournament: run 3's rule (tournament_channel_blocks) on the entries. A prefix of n
entries runs the doublings of top_k below n (at most D // dims rounds). Each round
adds the next window of `dims` adjacent channels of the summed q (windows()) to the
partial score and keeps the best top_k << (rounds left) entries, ties kept, so the
last round leaves top_k. One pool per query, not run 3's shuffled key blocks.

Entry e holds tokens [e m, (e + 1) m). Query t may pick it once finished,
e < (t + 1) // m, and only inside its own document, e m >= start (as the archived
csa2.top_picks and VarlenIndexer). No grad.

Shapes: q (S, H, D) for one packed row, kv (N, D), starts (S,) int64 document start
of each query. Returns entry (S, top_k) int64 and valid (S, top_k) bool; a short
prefix leaves invalid extras at entry 0.
"""

import torch
from torch import Tensor

from nanogpt.tournament_channel_blocks import windows
from nanogpt.tournament_fast import Settings

CHUNK = 2048  # queries per pass


def _allowed(starts: Tensor, m: int, a: int, b: int, lo: int, hi: int) -> Tensor:
    """(b - a, hi - lo) bool: queries a..b-1 may pick entries lo..hi-1."""
    t = torch.arange(a, b, device=starts.device)[:, None]
    e = torch.arange(lo, hi, device=starts.device)
    return (e * m >= starts[a:b, None]) & (e < (t + 1) // m)


def _halve(
    partials: list[Tensor], allowed: Tensor, doubles: Tensor, top_k: int
) -> Tensor:
    """(Q, E) summed partial scores, -inf for entries out after each query's rounds
    (`doubles` per query; partials holds the most rounds any query runs)."""
    score = torch.zeros(allowed.shape, device=allowed.device)
    alive = allowed
    for r, partial in enumerate(partials):
        score = score + partial
        live = score.masked_fill(~alive, float("-inf"))
        budget = top_k * 2 ** (doubles - r - 1).clamp(min=0)
        floor = live.sort(-1, descending=True).values.gather(
            -1, (budget - 1).clamp(max=live.shape[-1] - 1)[:, None]
        )
        alive = alive & ((r >= doubles)[:, None] | (live >= floor))
    return score.masked_fill(~alive, float("-inf"))


def _picks(
    q: Tensor, kv: Tensor, starts: Tensor, m: int, top_k: int, dims: int
) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:
    """exact (dims == 0) or tournament picks, CHUNK queries at a time."""
    length, dim = q.shape[0], q.shape[-1]
    entry = torch.zeros(length, top_k, dtype=torch.long, device=q.device)
    valid = torch.zeros(length, top_k, dtype=torch.bool, device=q.device)
    qs = q.float().sum(1)
    if dims:
        rounds = dim // dims
        values, order = windows(qs[:, None], Settings(top_k << rounds, top_k, dims))
    for a in range(0, length, CHUNK):
        b = min(a + CHUNK, length)
        lo, hi = -(-int(starts[a]) // m), min(b // m, kv.shape[0])  # starts ascend
        if hi <= lo:
            continue
        keys = kv[lo:hi].float()
        allowed = _allowed(starts, m, a, b, lo, hi)
        if dims:
            n = allowed.sum(-1)
            doubles = (
                n[:, None] > top_k * 2 ** torch.arange(rounds, device=n.device)
            ).sum(-1)
            window = [slice(r * dims, (r + 1) * dims) for r in range(rounds)]
            partials = [
                torch.zeros_like(qs[a:b]).scatter(
                    -1, order[0, a:b, w].long(), values[0, a:b, w]
                )
                @ keys.T
                for w in window[: int(doubles.max())]
            ]
            score = _halve(partials, allowed, doubles, top_k)
        else:
            score = (qs[a:b] @ keys.T).masked_fill(~allowed, float("-inf"))
        top, index = score.topk(min(top_k, hi - lo), -1)
        entry[a:b, : top.shape[1]] = index + lo
        valid[a:b, : top.shape[1]] = top > float("-inf")
    return entry, valid


@torch.library.custom_op("csa2_select::picks", mutates_args=())
def picks(
    q: Tensor, kv: Tensor, starts: Tensor, m: int, top_k: int, dims: int
) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:
    """exact (dims 0) or tournament (dims > 0) picks; opaque to torch.compile."""
    with torch.no_grad():
        return _picks(q, kv, starts, m, top_k, dims)


@picks.register_fake
def _(
    q: Tensor, kv: Tensor, starts: Tensor, m: int, top_k: int, dims: int
) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:
    shape = (q.shape[0], top_k)
    return (
        q.new_empty(shape, dtype=torch.long),
        q.new_empty(shape, dtype=torch.bool),
    )


def exact(
    q: Tensor, kv: Tensor, starts: Tensor, m: int, top_k: int = 64
) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:
    """Each query's top_k allowed entries by (sum_h q_h) . kv."""
    return picks(q.detach(), kv.detach(), starts, m, top_k, 0)


def tournament(
    q: Tensor, kv: Tensor, starts: Tensor, m: int, top_k: int = 64, dims: int = 16
) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:
    """Each query's top_k allowed entries by the halving rounds (module docstring)."""
    return picks(q.detach(), kv.detach(), starts, m, top_k, dims)


----------------------------------------
# scratch nanogpt.csa2_select_fast
----------------------------------------

"""csa2_select's picks (same rules, arguments and outputs) in one Triton kernel: one
program per query holds its allowed entries' scores in registers.

exact: the score (sum_h q_h) . kv over all channels, then the top_k by bisection on
the sortable score (block_topk_fast._floor), the lowest entries filling ties.
tournament: each round reads only the surviving entries' window of `dims` channels
(windows()), adds it to their scores and keeps the round's budget, ties kept, by
the same bisection; then the top_k as exact.

csa2_select sums every round its 2048-query chunk runs into the final ranking and
breaks ties in torch.topk's order; here each query ranks by its own rounds and the
lowest entries win ties. Picks agree except where scores tie. Valid picks come in
entry order (csa2_select: score order); invalid slots hold entry 0. Documents with
over WIDEST entries fall back to csa2_select.
"""

import torch
import triton
import triton.language as tl
from torch import Tensor

from nanogpt import csa2_select
from nanogpt.block_topk_fast import _floor
from nanogpt.tournament_channel_blocks import windows
from nanogpt.tournament_fast import Settings

# ruff: noqa: N803  (Triton's convention: upper-case constexpr arguments)

WIDEST = 2048  # most entries a query's program holds in registers


@triton.jit
def _sortable(score):
    """float32 -> int32 with the same order."""
    bits = score.to(tl.int32, bitcast=True)
    return tl.where(bits < 0, bits ^ 0x7FFFFFFF, bits)


@triton.jit
def _picks(
    q_ptr,
    kv_ptr,
    start_ptr,
    value_ptr,
    order_ptr,
    entry_ptr,
    valid_ptr,
    N,
    M: tl.constexpr,
    TOP: tl.constexpr,
    D: tl.constexpr,
    WIDTH: tl.constexpr,
    BD: tl.constexpr,
    L: tl.constexpr,
    R: tl.constexpr,
):
    """One program per query t: entries [ceil(start / M), min((t + 1) // M, N)),
    exact (R == 0) or R tournament rounds of L channels."""
    t = tl.program_id(0)
    lo = tl.cdiv(tl.load(start_ptr + t), M)
    n = tl.minimum((t + 1) // M, N) - lo
    slot = tl.arange(0, WIDTH)
    alive = slot < n
    rows = kv_ptr + (lo + slot)[:, None].to(tl.int64) * D
    score = tl.zeros((WIDTH,), tl.float32)
    if R == 0:
        for c0 in tl.static_range(0, D, BD):
            c = c0 + tl.arange(0, BD)
            q = tl.load(q_ptr + t * D + c)
            k = tl.load(rows + c[None, :], mask=alive[:, None], other=0.0)
            score += tl.sum(k.to(tl.float32) * q[None, :], 1)
    else:
        doubles = 0
        for r in tl.static_range(R):
            doubles += (n > (TOP << r)).to(tl.int32)
        for r in tl.static_range(R):
            if r < doubles:
                for c0 in tl.static_range(0, L, BD):
                    at = (t * R + r) * L + c0 + tl.arange(0, BD)
                    c = tl.load(order_ptr + at)
                    q = tl.load(value_ptr + at)
                    k = tl.load(rows + c[None, :], mask=alive[:, None], other=0.0)
                    score += tl.sum(k.to(tl.float32) * q[None, :], 1)
                key = _sortable(score)
                budget = tl.full((), TOP, tl.int32) << (doubles - r - 1)
                floor, few = _floor(key, alive, budget)
                alive = alive & (few | (key >= floor))
    key = _sortable(score)
    floor, few = _floor(key, alive, TOP)
    above = alive & (key > floor)
    tied = alive & (key == floor)
    need = TOP - tl.sum(above.to(tl.int32), 0)
    keep = (alive & few) | above | (tied & (tl.cumsum(tied.to(tl.int32), 0) <= need))
    rank = tl.cumsum(keep.to(tl.int32), 0) - 1
    out = t.to(tl.int64) * TOP + rank
    tl.store(entry_ptr + out, (lo + slot).to(tl.int64), mask=keep)
    tl.store(valid_ptr + out, 1, mask=keep)


def _run(
    q: Tensor, kv: Tensor, starts: Tensor, m: int, top_k: int, dims: int
) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:
    length, dim = q.shape[0], q.shape[-1]
    t = torch.arange(length, device=q.device)
    most = int(((t + 1) // m - (starts + m - 1) // m).max().clamp(min=1))
    width = max(16, triton.next_power_of_2(min(most, kv.shape[0])))
    if width > WIDEST:  # ponytail: long documents (eval) take csa2_select's path
        return csa2_select._picks(q, kv, starts, m, top_k, dims)
    entry = torch.zeros(length, top_k, dtype=torch.long, device=q.device)
    valid = torch.zeros(length, top_k, dtype=torch.int8, device=q.device)
    qs = q.float().sum(1).contiguous()
    rounds = dim // dims if dims else 0
    values, order = qs, qs  # unused by exact
    if dims:
        values, order = windows(qs[:, None], Settings(top_k << rounds, top_k, dims))
    _picks[(length,)](
        qs,
        kv.contiguous(),
        starts.contiguous(),
        values,
        order,
        entry,
        valid,
        kv.shape[0],
        M=m,
        TOP=top_k,
        D=dim,
        WIDTH=width,
        BD=min(dims or 16, max(1, 4096 // width)),
        L=dims,
        R=rounds,
        num_warps=4 if width <= 512 else 8,
    )
    return entry, valid.bool()


@torch.library.custom_op("csa2_select_fast::picks", mutates_args=())
def picks(
    q: Tensor, kv: Tensor, starts: Tensor, m: int, top_k: int, dims: int
) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:
    """exact (dims 0) or tournament (dims > 0) picks; opaque to torch.compile."""
    with torch.no_grad():
        return _run(q, kv, starts, m, top_k, dims)


@picks.register_fake
def _(
    q: Tensor, kv: Tensor, starts: Tensor, m: int, top_k: int, dims: int
) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:
    shape = (q.shape[0], top_k)
    return (
        q.new_empty(shape, dtype=torch.long),
        q.new_empty(shape, dtype=torch.bool),
    )


def exact(
    q: Tensor, kv: Tensor, starts: Tensor, m: int, top_k: int = 64
) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:
    """csa2_select.exact: each query's top_k allowed entries by (sum_h q_h) . kv.

    Args:
        q: (S, H, D) queries of one packed row.
        kv: (N, D) entries, entry e = tokens [e m, (e + 1) m).
        starts: (S,) int64 document start of each query.
        m: tokens per entry.
        top_k: picks per query.

    Returns:
        entry (S, top_k) int64 and valid (S, top_k) bool.
    """
    return picks(q.detach(), kv.detach(), starts, m, top_k, 0)


def tournament(
    q: Tensor, kv: Tensor, starts: Tensor, m: int, top_k: int = 64, dims: int = 16
) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:
    """csa2_select.tournament: halving rounds of `dims` channels (as exact, plus
    dims: channels per round)."""
    return picks(q.detach(), kv.detach(), starts, m, top_k, dims)


----------------------------------------
# scratch nanogpt.block_topk_fast
----------------------------------------

"""Exact top-k attention per block (block_topk.py's rule) in two Triton kernels:

    _scores   every query's full q.k (all channels) with each key of its document
              prefix, on tensor cores (bf16 operands, float32 accumulation; float32
              operands in IEEE float32), written in position order to a score buffer
    _select   per (query, block of tournament_fast's partition): the block's slot
              scores (in order with one block, through layouts()' table with
              several), the winners-th largest by bisection on the sortable key,
              then the scores above it and the lowest slots tied at it: exactly
              min(winners, alive) winners

Queries go through the buffer in parts of at most BUDGET floats. No grad: selection
only, as tournament_fast. Shapes, settings and mask as tournament_fast.
"""

import torch
import triton
import triton.language as tl
from torch import Tensor

from nanogpt.tournament_fast import (
    DEFAULT,
    TILE,
    Settings,
    _bits,
    attend,
    bit_mask,
    doc_tiles,
    layouts,
    window_words,
)

# ruff: noqa: N803  (Triton's convention: upper-case constexpr arguments)

BQ, BK = 64, 64  # queries and keys per score tile
SPAN = 1024  # keys per _scores program (a third grid axis: long prefixes in parallel)
BUDGET = 1 << 27  # score buffer floats (512 MiB)


@triton.jit
def _scores(
    q_ptr,
    k_ptr,
    start_ptr,
    s_ptr,
    Q0,
    C,
    W,
    sq_t,
    sq_h,
    sk_t,
    sk_h,
    D: tl.constexpr,
    BQ: tl.constexpr,
    BK: tl.constexpr,
    SPAN: tl.constexpr,
    PRECISION: tl.constexpr,
):
    """One program per (tile of BQ queries, head, SPAN keys): q.k for the span's
    keys from the tile's lowest document start to its last query, stored at (head,
    query, key - the query's document start) where the key is in the query's
    prefix."""
    rows = tl.program_id(0) * BQ + tl.arange(0, BQ)  # queries of this part
    head = tl.program_id(1)
    real = rows < C
    start = tl.load(start_ptr + rows, mask=real, other=0)
    t = Q0 + rows  # row positions
    d = tl.arange(0, D)
    q = tl.load(
        q_ptr + rows[:, None] * sq_t + head * sq_h + d[None, :],
        mask=real[:, None],
        other=0.0,
    )
    lo = tl.min(tl.where(real, start, 2147483647), 0)
    hi = tl.max(tl.where(real, t, 0), 0)
    out = s_ptr + head * C * W + rows[:, None] * W - start[:, None]
    begin = lo // BK * BK + tl.program_id(2) * SPAN
    for k0 in range(begin, tl.minimum(hi + 1, begin + SPAN), BK):
        pos = k0 + tl.arange(0, BK)
        k = tl.load(
            k_ptr + pos[:, None] * sk_t + head * sk_h + d[None, :],
            mask=(pos <= hi)[:, None],
            other=0.0,
        )
        s = tl.dot(q, tl.trans(k), input_precision=PRECISION)
        mine = (pos[None, :] >= start[:, None]) & (pos[None, :] <= t[:, None])
        tl.store(out + pos[None, :], s, mask=real[:, None] & mine)


@triton.jit
def _floor(key, alive, K):
    """The K-th largest alive key, or a value with exactly K alive keys at or above
    it (bisection between the alive min and max); and whether K or fewer are alive."""
    lo = tl.min(tl.where(alive, key, 2147483647), 0)
    hi = tl.max(tl.where(alive, key, -2147483648), 0)
    few = tl.sum(alive.to(tl.int32), 0) <= K
    active = (~few) & (lo < hi)
    while active:
        mid = (lo >> 1) + (hi >> 1) + ((lo | hi) & 1)  # ceil((lo + hi) / 2)
        count = tl.sum(((key >= mid) & alive).to(tl.int32), 0)
        lo = tl.where(count >= K, mid, lo)
        hi = tl.where(count >= K, hi, mid - 1)
        hi = tl.where(count == K, mid, hi)
        active = lo < hi
    return lo, few


@triton.jit
def _select(
    s_ptr,
    start_ptr,
    layout_ptr,
    won_ptr,
    Q0,
    C,
    C0,
    TOTAL,
    W,
    NB,
    most_blocks,
    BLOCK: tl.constexpr,
    QUOTA: tl.constexpr,
    WIDTH: tl.constexpr,
):
    """One program per (head and query, block), the blocks of a row adjacent: the
    block's QUOTA winners if its slots fit WIDTH (one block: WIDTH the power of 2
    at or above n, at least QUOTA; several: WIDTH = BLOCK), into won (H, TOTAL,
    most_blocks * QUOTA) at query C0 + its index in this part."""
    blk = tl.program_id(0) % NB
    row = tl.program_id(0) // NB  # head * C + query of this part
    start = tl.load(start_ptr + row % C)
    n = Q0 + row % C - start + 1  # keys in the query's prefix
    blocks = tl.cdiv(n, BLOCK)
    fits = (n <= WIDTH) & ((2 * n > WIDTH) | (WIDTH == QUOTA))
    if (blk < blocks) & (((blocks > 1) & (WIDTH == BLOCK)) | ((blocks == 1) & fits)):
        slot = tl.arange(0, WIDTH)
        pos = slot
        if WIDTH == BLOCK:  # several blocks: layouts()' table
            table = layout_ptr + BLOCK * ((blocks - 1) * blocks // 2 - 1)
            shuffled = tl.load(table + blk * BLOCK + slot, mask=blocks > 1, other=0)
            pos = tl.where(blocks > 1, shuffled, slot)
        alive = pos < n
        score = tl.load(s_ptr + row * W + pos, mask=alive, other=float("-inf"))
        bits = score.to(tl.int32, bitcast=True)
        key = tl.where(bits < 0, bits ^ 0x7FFFFFFF, bits)  # float order as int order
        floor, few = _floor(key, alive, QUOTA)
        above = alive & (key > floor)
        tied = alive & (key == floor)
        need = QUOTA - tl.sum(above.to(tl.int32), 0)
        lowest = tl.cumsum(tied.to(tl.int32), 0) <= need
        keep = (alive & few) | above | (tied & lowest)
        rank = tl.cumsum(keep.to(tl.int32), 0) - 1
        head = row // C
        out = won_ptr + ((head * TOTAL + C0 + row % C) * most_blocks + blk) * QUOTA
        tl.store(out + rank, start + pos, mask=keep)


@torch.no_grad()
def select(
    q: Tensor,
    k: Tensor,
    cu_seqlens: Tensor,
    longest: int,
    settings: Settings = DEFAULT,
    first: int = 0,
) -> Tensor:
    """Each query's winners, row positions, -1 none (tournament_fast.select's
    arguments and shape)."""
    length, heads, dim = q.shape
    assert q.stride(-1) == 1 and k.stride(-1) == 1, "channels must be contiguous"
    block, quota = settings.block, settings.winners
    blocks = triton.cdiv(longest, block)
    width = blocks * block  # score columns per query
    queries = torch.arange(
        first, first + length, device=q.device, dtype=cu_seqlens.dtype
    )
    starts = cu_seqlens[torch.searchsorted(cu_seqlens, queries, right=True) - 1]
    starts = starts.int()
    won = torch.full(
        (heads, length, blocks * quota), -1, dtype=torch.int32, device=q.device
    )
    table = layouts(blocks, block, str(q.device), True)
    step = max(BQ, BUDGET // (heads * width) // BQ * BQ)
    scores = torch.empty(heads * min(step, length) * width, device=q.device)
    exact = q.dtype == torch.float32
    for c in range(0, length, step):
        part = min(step, length - c)
        spans = triton.cdiv(width + 2 * BK, SPAN)  # a tile's keys: <= width + 2 BK
        _scores[(triton.cdiv(part, BQ), heads, spans)](
            q[c:],
            k,
            starts[c:],
            scores,
            first + c,
            part,
            width,
            q.stride(0),
            q.stride(1),
            k.stride(0),
            k.stride(1),
            D=dim,
            BQ=BQ,
            BK=BK,
            SPAN=SPAN,
            PRECISION="ieee" if exact else "tf32",
            num_warps=4,
            num_stages=1 if exact else 2,
        )
        widths = [quota << i for i in range((block // quota).bit_length())]
        for w in widths:
            nb = blocks if w == block else 1
            _select[(heads * part * nb,)](
                scores,
                starts[c:],
                table,
                won,
                first + c,
                part,
                c,
                length,
                width,
                nb,
                blocks,
                BLOCK=block,
                QUOTA=quota,
                WIDTH=w,
                num_warps=4 if w >= 2048 else 1,  # tuned: topk_select_tune.csv
            )
    return won


@torch.library.custom_op("block_topk_fast::picked", mutates_args=())
def picked(
    q: Tensor,
    k: Tensor,
    cu_seqlens: Tensor,
    low: Tensor,
    longest: int,
    block: int,
    winners: int,
    dims: int,
    first: int = 0,
) -> Tensor:
    """tournament_fast.picked with this module's select()."""
    won = select(q, k, cu_seqlens, longest, Settings(block, winners, dims), first)
    heads, length, most = won.shape
    words = window_words(k.shape[0], longest)
    assert heads * length * words < 2**31, "int32 offsets overflow: pass a chunk"
    out = torch.zeros(heads, length, words, dtype=torch.int32, device=q.device)
    _bits[(heads * length,)](
        won, low, out, length, most, words, TILE=TILE, MP=triton.next_power_of_2(most)
    )
    return out


@picked.register_fake
def _(
    q: Tensor,
    k: Tensor,
    cu_seqlens: Tensor,
    low: Tensor,
    longest: int,
    block: int,
    winners: int,
    dims: int,
    first: int = 0,
) -> Tensor:
    length, heads, _ = q.shape
    words = window_words(k.shape[0], longest)
    return q.new_empty(heads, length, words, dtype=torch.int32)


def attention(
    q: Tensor,
    k: Tensor,
    v: Tensor,
    cu_seqlens: Tensor,
    scale: float,
    longest: int | None = None,
    settings: Settings = DEFAULT,
    chunk: int | None = None,
) -> Tensor:
    """tournament_fast.attention on this module's winners."""
    length, heads, _ = q.shape
    step = chunk or length
    assert length % TILE == 0 and step % TILE == 0, (length, step)
    bound = length if longest is None else min(longest, length)
    low, count, index = doc_tiles(cu_seqlens, length, heads)
    outs = []
    for first in reversed(range(0, length, step)):  # as tournament_fast.attention
        end = min(first + step, length)
        tiles = slice(first // TILE, end // TILE)
        words = picked(
            q[first:end].detach(),
            k.detach(),
            cu_seqlens,
            low[tiles],
            bound,
            settings.block,
            settings.winners,
            settings.dims,
            first,
        )
        mask_mod = bit_mask(words, low[tiles])
        part = q[first:end], k, v, scale, count[..., tiles], index[..., tiles, :]
        outs.append(attend(*part, mask_mod))
    return outs[0] if len(outs) == 1 else torch.cat(outs[::-1])


----------------------------------------
# scratch nanogpt.csa2_window
----------------------------------------

"""CSA2's window for record 91: per token, G shared K=V vectors instead of each head's
own K and V (DeepSeek-V4's window is one K=V head for all query heads). Each is
made like a CSA2 entry with m = 1: e = rms_norm(x w_g); its key is e rotated by the
token's RoPE row (csa2_flex.rotate), its value e itself, so a decode cache keeps G x
128 channels per window token (record 91: 6 x (96 + 128)). Heads [g H / G,
(g + 1) H / G) read vector g.
"""

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch import Tensor


def shared_window(
    x: Tensor, w: tuple[Tensor, ...], heads: int, factor1: Tensor, factor2: Tensor
) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:
    """(T, heads, D) window keys and values of x (T, C) from the G weights w (D, C):
    each group's vector rms-normed, keys rotated as csa2_flex.rotate."""
    length, groups = x.shape[0], len(w)
    e = F.rms_norm(F.linear(x, torch.cat(w)).view(length, groups, -1), (w[0].shape[0],))
    width = factor1.shape[1]
    y = e[..., :width].float()
    flip = y.unflatten(-1, (-1, 2)).flip(-1).flatten(-2)
    f1, f2 = factor1[:length, None].float(), factor2[:length, None].float()
    k = torch.cat([(f1 * y + f2 * flip).type_as(e), e[..., width:]], -1)
    return k.repeat_interleave(heads // groups, 1), e.repeat_interleave(
        heads // groups, 1
    )


----------------------------------------
# scratch nanogpt.tournament_fast
----------------------------------------

"""Tournament attention for training, fast: nanogpt/tournament.py's method at any
settings, the same winners at its settings.

Each query runs the tournament on its own document's prefix: blocks of `block` key
slots in the emulation's random slot order, each sending `winners` keys to
attention; each round adds the query's next `dims` channels by |q| and every block
keeps its top half. attention() is exact attention on the winners (FlexAttention).

Differences from tournament.py, none of which change a winner:
- one kernel orders each query's channels by |q| and keeps only those rounds read;
- rounds run on a vector that halves each round (survivors packed); a block that
  ties at a round's threshold is listed and rerun at full width afterwards;
- the widest launch spans the blocks of `longest`, the caller's bound on document
  length, instead of the whole row;
- winners become bit words over each 128-query tile's document window instead of
  the whole row, and flex's block mask comes from the document starts instead of
  a scan of every bit;
- a query whose prefix spans several blocks reads a copy of its document's keys in
  its layout's slot order (contiguous loads, not one per slot).
Triton kernels autotune on the device (warps, threshold method per round width).
"""

import itertools
from collections.abc import Callable, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from functools import cache
from typing import cast

import torch
import triton
import triton.language as tl
from torch import Tensor
from torch.nn.attention.flex_attention import (
    BlockMask,
    FlexKernelOptions,
    flex_attention,
)

from attention.triton.common import DIMS, _slots, _top
from nanogpt.tournament import BLOCK, QUOTA, _growing_rounds, _slot_positions

# ruff: noqa: N803  (Triton's convention: upper-case constexpr arguments)

TILE = 128  # flex block-mask tile, queries and keys
CAP = 1024  # tied blocks rerun per launch width (measured: at most 8 per call)
REPEATS = 25  # autotune timings per config


def _bench(
    kernel_call: Callable[[], object], quantiles: Sequence[float]
) -> list[float]:
    """Autotune timing as the kernels run in select(): after ~0.2 s of GPU work (the
    GPU idles down while a config compiles, up to a minute) and on a warm L2 (each
    launch rereads what the one before read; triton's do_bench flushes L2)."""
    kernel_call()  # compiles
    a = torch.empty(4096, 4096, device="cuda")
    for _ in range(50):
        a @ a
    events = [
        [torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True) for _ in range(2)] for _ in range(REPEATS)
    ]
    for start, end in events:
        start.record()
        kernel_call()
        end.record()
    torch.cuda.synchronize()
    times = torch.tensor([start.elapsed_time(end) for start, end in events])
    return times.quantile(torch.tensor(quantiles)).tolist()


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Settings:
    """Key slots per block, keys each block sends to attention, channels each
    round adds; all powers of 2."""

    block: int = BLOCK
    winners: int = QUOTA
    dims: int = DIMS

    @property
    def rounds(self) -> int:
        """Halving rounds of a full block."""
        return (self.block // self.winners).bit_length() - 1

    @property
    def channels(self) -> int:
        """Channels the rounds of a full block read."""
        return self.rounds * self.dims


DEFAULT = Settings()


@cache
def layouts(blocks: int, block: int, device: str, ordered: bool) -> Tensor:
    """Slot -> document position tables for 2 to `blocks` blocks of `block` slots,
    concatenated: the emulation's random order (as tournament.layouts), each
    block's slots in position order when `ordered` (the same keys meet)."""
    tables = []
    for b in range(2, max(blocks, 2) + 1):
        perm = torch.randperm(block * b, generator=torch.Generator().manual_seed(0))
        tables.append(
            perm.view(b, block).sort(-1).values.flatten() if ordered else perm
        )
    return torch.cat(tables).int().to(device)


@triton.autotune(
    configs=[triton.Config({}, num_warps=w) for w in (1, 2, 4)],
    key=["T", "CHANNELS"],
    cache_results=True,
    do_bench=_bench,
)
@triton.jit
def _order(q_ptr, qo_ptr, order_ptr, T, H, D: tl.constexpr, CHANNELS: tl.constexpr):
    """One program per (head, query) row: its first CHANNELS channels by
    descending |q| (ties: lower channel first, as a stable argsort) and q there."""
    row = tl.program_id(0)
    c = tl.arange(0, D)
    x = tl.load(q_ptr + ((row % T) * H + row // T) * D + c).to(tl.float32)
    size = tl.abs(x).to(tl.int32, bitcast=True).to(tl.int64)  # int order = |q| order
    ranked = tl.sort((size << 32) | (D - 1 - c), descending=True)
    channel = (D - 1 - (ranked & 0xFFFFFFFF)).to(tl.int32)
    first = c < CHANNELS
    tl.store(order_ptr + row * CHANNELS + c, channel, mask=first)
    tl.store(qo_ptr + row * CHANNELS + c, tl.gather(x, channel, 0), mask=first)


@triton.jit
def _top_sort(score, alive, budget, WIDTH: tl.constexpr):
    """_top by sorting: alive entries at or above the budget-th largest alive score;
    every alive entry if budget or fewer are."""
    s = tl.where(alive, score, float("-inf"))
    ranked = tl.sort(s, descending=True)
    floor = tl.sum(tl.where(tl.arange(0, WIDTH) == budget - 1, ranked, 0.0), 0)
    few = tl.sum(alive.to(tl.int32), 0) <= budget
    return alive & ((s >= floor) | few)


@triton.jit
def _pack(keep, pos, dot, WIDTH: tl.constexpr, LOG: tl.constexpr):
    """The first WIDTH / 2 kept entries in slot order (binary search on the running
    count): whether each exists, its position and partial logit; and how many
    entries were kept."""
    count = tl.cumsum(keep.to(tl.int32), 0)
    want = tl.arange(0, WIDTH // 2) + 1
    at = tl.zeros((WIDTH // 2,), tl.int32)  # slots whose count is below want
    for t in tl.static_range(LOG):
        step = WIDTH >> (t + 1)
        at = tl.where(tl.gather(count, at + (step - 1), 0) < want, at + step, at)
    total = tl.sum(keep.to(tl.int32), 0)
    return want <= total, tl.gather(pos, at, 0), tl.gather(dot, at, 0), total


def _distinct(
    configs: list[triton.Config], named_args: dict, **kwargs: object
) -> list[triton.Config]:
    """Configs that differ at this launch width: 1 to 16 slots per thread (or one
    warp), and sort thresholds up to twice the width (all larger sort every round)."""
    width = cast(int, {**named_args, **kwargs}["WIDTH"])
    seen, out = set(), []
    for config in configs:
        slots = width // (32 * config.num_warps)
        sort_below = min(config.kwargs["SORT_BELOW"], 2 * width)
        fits = (slots >= 1 or config.num_warps == 1) and slots <= 16
        if fits and (config.num_warps, sort_below) not in seen:
            seen.add((config.num_warps, sort_below))
            out.append(config)
    return out


@triton.autotune(
    configs=[
        triton.Config({"SORT_BELOW": s}, num_warps=w)
        for w in (1, 2, 4, 8)
        for s in (256, 512, 1024, 2048)
    ],
    key=["C", "WIDTH", "BLOCK", "QUOTA", "DIMS"],
    prune_configs_by={"early_config_prune": _distinct},
    reset_to_zero=["ties_ptr"],
    cache_results=True,
    do_bench=_bench,
)
@triton.jit
def _rounds(
    q_ptr,
    order_ptr,
    kt_ptr,
    off_ptr,
    start_ptr,
    layout_ptr,
    won_ptr,
    ties_ptr,
    tied_ptr,
    T,
    Q0,
    C,
    most_blocks,
    D: tl.constexpr,
    CHANNELS: tl.constexpr,
    DIMS: tl.constexpr,
    ROUNDS: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK: tl.constexpr,
    QUOTA: tl.constexpr,
    CAP: tl.constexpr,
    WIDTH: tl.constexpr,
    R: tl.constexpr,
    LOG: tl.constexpr,
    SORT_BELOW: tl.constexpr,
):
    """One program per (head and query, block) whose block has WIDTH slots, for the
    C queries at row positions Q0 on of a row of T keys: its R rounds on a halving
    vector (threshold by radix select, or by sort below SORT_BELOW slots), then its
    QUOTA winners. A block that tied at a threshold lists itself for _redo. Keys:
    kt (H, D, T) from the query's document start, or with several blocks from its
    copy in slot order at off (key_copies())."""
    row = tl.program_id(0)  # head * C + query - Q0
    blk = tl.program_id(1)
    head = row // C
    start = tl.load(start_ptr + row % C)
    n = Q0 + row % C - start + 1  # keys in the query's cache
    blocks = tl.cdiv(n, BLOCK)
    rounds = _growing_rounds(n, blocks, QUOTA, ROUNDS)
    if (blk < blocks) & ((QUOTA << rounds) == WIDTH):
        pos = _slot_positions(layout_ptr, blocks, blk, WIDTH, BLOCK)
        alive = pos < n
        copy = blocks > 1
        here = start
        if WIDTH == BLOCK:  # pos: slots in the copy until the rounds end
            here = tl.where(copy, tl.load(off_ptr + row % C, mask=copy, other=0), start)
            pos = tl.where(copy, blk * BLOCK + tl.arange(0, WIDTH), pos)
        keys = kt_ptr + head * D * T + here
        dot = tl.zeros((WIDTH,), tl.float32)  # partial logits
        ties = 0
        for r in tl.static_range(R):
            for j in tl.static_range(DIMS):
                i = r * DIMS + j
                d = tl.load(order_ptr + row * CHANNELS + i)
                k = tl.load(keys + pos + d * T, mask=alive, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
                dot += tl.load(q_ptr + row * CHANNELS + i) * k
            score = tl.where(alive, dot, float("-inf"))
            if (WIDTH >> r) >= SORT_BELOW:
                keep = _top(score, alive, WIDTH >> (r + 1))
            else:
                keep = _top_sort(score, alive, WIDTH >> (r + 1), WIDTH >> r)
            alive, pos, dot, total = _pack(keep, pos, dot, WIDTH >> r, LOG - r)
            ties += (total > (WIDTH >> (r + 1))).to(tl.int32)
        if WIDTH == BLOCK:  # slots in the copy back to document positions
            table = layout_ptr + BLOCK * ((blocks - 1) * blocks // 2 - 1)
            pos = tl.where(copy, tl.load(table + pos, mask=alive & copy, other=0), pos)
        out = won_ptr + (row * most_blocks + blk) * QUOTA + tl.arange(0, QUOTA)
        tl.store(out, start + pos, mask=alive)
        if ties > 0:
            at = tl.atomic_add(ties_ptr, 1)
            tl.store(tied_ptr + 2 * at, row, mask=at < CAP)
            tl.store(tied_ptr + 2 * at + 1, blk, mask=at < CAP)


@triton.jit
def _redo(
    q_ptr,
    order_ptr,
    kt_ptr,
    start_ptr,
    layout_ptr,
    won_ptr,
    ties_ptr,
    tied_ptr,
    T,
    Q0,
    C,
    most_blocks,
    D: tl.constexpr,
    CHANNELS: tl.constexpr,
    DIMS: tl.constexpr,
    ROUNDS: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK: tl.constexpr,
    QUOTA: tl.constexpr,
    CAP: tl.constexpr,
):
    """One program per listed (row, block) of launch width QUOTA << (axis 1 + 1):
    its winners again by tournament._query_rounds' full-width rounds, on the
    emulation's slot order (unsorted layout), as tournament.py picks them."""
    item = tl.program_id(0)
    launch = tl.program_id(1) + 1  # the launch of width QUOTA << launch
    if item < tl.minimum(tl.load(ties_ptr + launch), CAP):
        listed = tied_ptr + (launch * CAP + item) * 2
        row = tl.load(listed)
        blk = tl.load(listed + 1)
        head = row // C
        start = tl.load(start_ptr + row % C)
        n = Q0 + row % C - start + 1
        blocks = tl.cdiv(n, BLOCK)
        rounds = _growing_rounds(n, blocks, QUOTA, ROUNDS)
        pos = _slot_positions(layout_ptr, blocks, blk, BLOCK, BLOCK)
        alive = pos < n  # a narrower block is a single block: slots = positions
        keys = kt_ptr + head * D * T + start + pos
        dot = tl.zeros((BLOCK,), tl.float32)
        for r in range(rounds):
            for j in tl.static_range(DIMS):
                i = r * DIMS + j
                d = tl.load(order_ptr + row * CHANNELS + i)
                k = tl.load(keys + d * T, mask=alive, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
                dot += tl.load(q_ptr + row * CHANNELS + i) * k
            score = tl.where(alive, dot, float("-inf"))
            alive = _top(score, alive, (QUOTA << rounds) >> (r + 1))
        rank = _slots(alive)
        out = won_ptr + (row * most_blocks + blk) * QUOTA
        tl.store(out + tl.arange(0, QUOTA), -1)
        tl.debug_barrier()
        tl.store(out + rank, start + pos, mask=alive & (rank < QUOTA))


def select(
    q: Tensor,
    k: Tensor,
    cu_seqlens: Tensor,
    longest: int,
    settings: Settings = DEFAULT,
    first: int = 0,
) -> Tensor:
    """Each query's winners, row positions, -1 none.

    Args:
        q: (C, H, D) the queries at row positions first to first + C - 1.
        k: (T, H, D) one packed row.
        cu_seqlens: document starts, may repeat T at its end.
        longest: bound on every document's length, in tokens.
        settings: block, winners and channels per round.
        first: row position of the first query.

    Returns:
        (H, C, winners * ceil(longest / block)) int32.
    """
    length, heads, dim = q.shape
    assert settings.channels <= dim, settings
    rows = heads * length
    blocks = triton.cdiv(longest, settings.block)
    query = torch.empty(heads, length, settings.channels, device=q.device)
    order = torch.empty_like(query, dtype=torch.int32)
    _order[(rows,)](
        q.contiguous(), query, order, length, heads, D=dim, CHANNELS=settings.channels
    )
    queries = torch.arange(
        first, first + length, device=q.device, dtype=cu_seqlens.dtype
    )
    starts = cu_seqlens[torch.searchsorted(cu_seqlens, queries, right=True) - 1]
    won = torch.full(
        (heads, length, blocks * settings.winners),
        -1,
        dtype=torch.int32,
        device=q.device,
    )
    ties = torch.zeros(settings.rounds + 1, dtype=torch.int32, device=q.device)
    tied = torch.empty(settings.rounds + 1, CAP, 2, dtype=torch.int32, device=q.device)
    device = str(q.device)
    table = layouts(blocks, settings.block, device, True)
    kt = k.permute(1, 2, 0).contiguous()  # (H, D, T)
    off = torch.zeros(1, dtype=torch.int32, device=q.device)
    if longest > settings.block:  # some prefix may span several blocks
        kt, off = key_copies(kt, cu_seqlens, first, length, settings.block, table)
    assert kt.numel() < 2**31, "int32 offsets overflow: pass a chunk"
    args = {
        "q_ptr": query,
        "order_ptr": order,
        "kt_ptr": kt,
        "start_ptr": starts.int(),
        "won_ptr": won,
        "T": kt.shape[-1],
        "Q0": first,
        "C": length,
        "most_blocks": blocks,
        "D": dim,
        "CHANNELS": settings.channels,
        "DIMS": settings.dims,
        "ROUNDS": settings.rounds,
        "BLOCK": settings.block,
        "QUOTA": settings.winners,
        "CAP": CAP,
    }
    for r in range(settings.rounds + 1):
        width = settings.winners << r
        grid = (rows, blocks if width == settings.block else 1)
        _rounds[grid](
            **args,
            off_ptr=off,
            layout_ptr=table,
            ties_ptr=ties[r],
            tied_ptr=tied[r],
            WIDTH=width,
            R=r,
            LOG=width.bit_length() - 1,
        )
    if settings.rounds:  # widths of one slot per winner have no rounds, no ties
        _redo[(CAP, settings.rounds)](
            **args,
            layout_ptr=layouts(blocks, settings.block, device, False),
            ties_ptr=ties,
            tied_ptr=tied,
            num_warps=max(1, min(8, settings.block // 256)),
        )
    return won


def key_copies(
    kt: Tensor, cu_seqlens: Tensor, first: int, length: int, block: int, table: Tensor
) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:
    """kt (H, D, T) followed by one copy of a document's keys in slot order for each
    (document, block count of 2 or more) among the queries first to first + length
    - 1 (table: layouts()' ordered tables); and each query's copy offset in the
    result (0 if none). A block of a copy is contiguous; in kt its slots are about
    `blocks` keys apart, one 32-byte sector per slot from 16 blocks on."""
    rows = kt.shape[-1]
    bounds = sorted({*cu_seqlens.tolist(), rows})
    parts, off, at = [kt], torch.zeros(length, dtype=torch.int32), rows
    for start, end in itertools.pairwise(bounds):
        low, high = max(start, first), min(end, first + length)  # queries here
        least = max(2, triton.cdiv(low - start + 1, block))
        for blocks in range(least, triton.cdiv(high - start, block) + 1):
            a = max(low, start + (blocks - 1) * block)  # queries with `blocks` blocks
            z = min(high, start + blocks * block)
            if a < z:
                slots = table[block * ((blocks - 1) * blocks // 2 - 1) :]
                pos = (start + slots[: blocks * block]).clamp(max=rows - 1)
                parts.append(kt[:, :, pos])
                off[a - first : z - first] = at
                at += blocks * block
    return torch.cat(parts, -1) if len(parts) > 1 else kt, off.to(kt.device)


def window_words(length: int, longest: int) -> int:
    """Bit words per query: a 128-query tile's keys start at most longest + 254
    before its end (its first query's document start, rounded down to a tile)."""
    return min(triton.cdiv(longest + 2 * TILE, 32), triton.cdiv(length, 32))


@triton.autotune(
    configs=[triton.Config({}, num_warps=w) for w in (1, 2, 4, 8)],
    key=["T", "MP"],
    cache_results=True,
    do_bench=_bench,
)
@triton.jit
def _bits(
    won_ptr,
    low_ptr,
    words_ptr,
    T,
    M,
    WORDS,
    TILE: tl.constexpr,
    MP: tl.constexpr,
):
    """One program per (head, query) row of zeroed words: bit s % 32 of word s // 32
    is set when key base + s is a winner, base = the first key of the row's tile's
    window. One atomic or per winner (rerunning sets the same bits)."""
    row = tl.program_id(0)
    base = tl.load(low_ptr + (row % T) // TILE) * TILE
    m = tl.arange(0, MP)
    key = tl.load(won_ptr + row * M + m, mask=m < M, other=-1)
    at = key - base
    bit = (1 << (at % 32)).to(tl.int32)
    word = words_ptr + row * WORDS + at // 32
    tl.atomic_or(word, bit, mask=key >= 0, sem="relaxed")


# An opaque op: built inside a compiled graph, a scattered mask reached flex's
# kernel all False (tournament.py)
@torch.library.custom_op("tournament_fast::picked", mutates_args=())
def picked(
    q: Tensor,
    k: Tensor,
    cu_seqlens: Tensor,
    low: Tensor,
    longest: int,
    block: int,
    winners: int,
    dims: int,
    first: int = 0,
) -> Tensor:
    """(H, C, window_words) int32: select()'s winners for the C queries q from row
    position first, as bits over each query tile's window, which starts at key tile
    low[tile] (low: those queries' tiles)."""
    won = select(q, k, cu_seqlens, longest, Settings(block, winners, dims), first)
    heads, length, most = won.shape
    words = window_words(k.shape[0], longest)
    assert heads * length * words < 2**31, "int32 offsets overflow: pass a chunk"
    out = torch.zeros(heads, length, words, dtype=torch.int32, device=q.device)
    _bits[(heads * length,)](
        won, low, out, length, most, words, TILE=TILE, MP=triton.next_power_of_2(most)
    )
    return out


@picked.register_fake
def _(
    q: Tensor,
    k: Tensor,
    cu_seqlens: Tensor,
    low: Tensor,
    longest: int,
    block: int,
    winners: int,
    dims: int,
    first: int = 0,
) -> Tensor:
    length, heads, _ = q.shape
    words = window_words(k.shape[0], longest)
    return q.new_empty(heads, length, words, dtype=torch.int32)


# Opaque too: computed in the graph, the mask's low reaches flex's lowering without a
# fixed layout, which fails in no-grad graphs (torch 2.10: validation)
@torch.library.custom_op("tournament_fast::tiles", mutates_args=())
def tiles(cu_seqlens: Tensor, length: int) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor, Tensor]:
    """Each 128-query tile's first key tile (its first query's document start),
    its KV tile count and its KV tile indices (tiles, tiles): every tile from that
    one to the diagonal, all partial."""
    n = length // TILE
    tile = torch.arange(n, device=cu_seqlens.device)
    first = (TILE * tile).to(cu_seqlens.dtype)
    start = cu_seqlens[torch.searchsorted(cu_seqlens, first, right=True) - 1]
    low = (start // TILE).long()
    index = (low[:, None] + tile[None]).clamp(max=n - 1).int()
    return low, (tile - low + 1).int(), index


@tiles.register_fake
def _(cu_seqlens: Tensor, length: int) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor, Tensor]:
    n = length // TILE
    return (
        cu_seqlens.new_empty(n, dtype=torch.long),
        cu_seqlens.new_empty(n, dtype=torch.int32),
        cu_seqlens.new_empty(n, n, dtype=torch.int32),
    )


def doc_tiles(cu_seqlens: Tensor, length: int, heads: int) -> tuple[Tensor, ...]:
    """tiles() with the counts and indices broadcast to flex's (1, H, tiles) and
    (1, H, tiles, tiles)."""
    low, count, index = tiles(cu_seqlens, length)
    n = length // TILE
    return low, count.expand(1, heads, n), index.expand(1, heads, n, n)


SWEPT = {  # fastest forward, then backward, at stage 3 on an RTX 4070 Ti SUPER
    "fwd_BLOCK_M": 128,
    "fwd_BLOCK_N": 32,
    "fwd_num_warps": 8,
    "fwd_num_stages": 3,
    "bwd_BLOCK_M1": 32,
    "bwd_BLOCK_N1": 32,
    "bwd_BLOCK_M2": 32,
    "bwd_BLOCK_N2": 32,
    "bwd_num_warps": 4,
    "bwd_num_stages": 2,
}


def flex_options() -> FlexKernelOptions:
    """Flex kernel options for this GPU: on Ada (sm_89) the swept tiles (forward
    + backward 1.14 -> 0.84 ms at stage 3); elsewhere torch's defaults, with a
    2-stage forward where the 3-stage default (112 KB) exceeds the shared memory
    per block."""
    props = torch.cuda.get_device_properties()
    small = props.shared_memory_per_block_optin < 112 * 1024  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    ada = (props.major, props.minor) == (8, 9)
    # the typed options lack fwd_ keys, which flex reads
    options = SWEPT if ada else {"fwd_num_stages": 2} if small else {}
    return cast(FlexKernelOptions, options)


def attention(
    q: Tensor,
    k: Tensor,
    v: Tensor,
    cu_seqlens: Tensor,
    scale: float,
    longest: int | None = None,
    settings: Settings = DEFAULT,
    chunk: int | None = None,
) -> Tensor:
    """Each query's exact attention on its select() winners.

    Args:
        q, k, v: (T, H, D) one packed row, T a multiple of 128.
        cu_seqlens: document starts, may repeat T at its end.
        scale: logit scale.
        longest: bound on every document's length; None: T.
        settings: block, winners and channels per round.
        chunk: queries selected and attended at a time, a multiple of 128 (memory
            for the winners scales with chunk * T, not T^2); None: T. Same output.

    Returns:
        (T, H, D) attention output.
    """
    length, heads, _ = q.shape
    step = chunk or length
    assert length % TILE == 0 and step % TILE == 0, (length, step)
    bound = length if longest is None else min(longest, length)
    low, count, index = doc_tiles(cu_seqlens, length, heads)
    outs = []
    # last chunk first: its key copies are the largest, later (smaller) ones reuse
    # the cached memory; in increasing size each chunk reserved a new segment
    # (13 GiB reserved for 1.2 GB used at T = 131072, then CUDA out of memory)
    for first in reversed(range(0, length, step)):
        end = min(first + step, length)
        tiles = slice(first // TILE, end // TILE)
        words = picked(
            q[first:end].detach(),
            k.detach(),
            cu_seqlens,
            low[tiles],
            bound,
            settings.block,
            settings.winners,
            settings.dims,
            first,
        )
        mask_mod = bit_mask(words, low[tiles])
        part = q[first:end], k, v, scale, count[..., tiles], index[..., tiles, :]
        outs.append(attend(*part, mask_mod))
    return outs[0] if len(outs) == 1 else torch.cat(outs[::-1])


MaskMod = Callable[[Tensor, Tensor, Tensor, Tensor], Tensor]  # flex mask_mod


def bit_mask(words: Tensor, low: Tensor) -> MaskMod:
    """Flex mask_mod: the key is one of the query's winners in words (picked())."""

    def winner(b: Tensor, h: Tensor, q_idx: Tensor, kv_idx: Tensor) -> Tensor:
        at = kv_idx - low[q_idx // TILE] * TILE
        return ((words[h, q_idx, at // 32] >> (at % 32)) & 1) != 0

    return winner


def attend(
    q: Tensor,
    k: Tensor,
    v: Tensor,
    scale: float,
    count: Tensor,
    index: Tensor,
    mask_mod: MaskMod,
) -> Tensor:
    """Flex attention of the queries q (C, H, D) on the packed row k, v (T, H, D)
    over the KV tiles index (count per query tile, doc_tiles()) where mask_mod
    holds."""
    lengths = q.shape[0], k.shape[0]
    mask = BlockMask.from_kv_blocks(
        count, index, BLOCK_SIZE=TILE, mask_mod=mask_mod, seq_lengths=lengths
    )
    q, k, v = (x.transpose(0, 1)[None] for x in (q, k, v))
    out = flex_attention(
        q, k, v, block_mask=mask, scale=scale, kernel_options=flex_options()
    )
    return cast(Tensor, out)[0].transpose(0, 1)


----------------------------------------
# scratch nanogpt.tournament_channel_blocks
----------------------------------------

"""Tournament attention for training with contiguous channel blocks: tournament_fast's
method where each round adds one contiguous block of `dims` channels instead of the
query's next `dims` channels by single-channel |q|.

Round r's block, per head and query: of the windows of `dims` adjacent channels
holding no channel an earlier round read, the one with the largest sum of |q|
(lowest start on ties), if that sum is positive; otherwise the window with the
largest |q| sum over unread channels, its already read channels weighted 0 (no
channel counts twice). Zero channels (record 91 pads q, k from 96 to 128 with
zeros) never win a round while a positive free window exists.
